In Dakha,      Bangladesh,   a   simple village   dispute   over
damage caused by a hapless, wandering cow ended in
totally irrational bloodshed and arson.
Five people wound up dead, and three were critically
injured, around the village or Gopingar when the cow,
owned by one Nural Islam, decided to indulge on a spot of
grazing in the rice fields of a neighbour, who later claimed
the animal had damaged his land.
When Islam refused to pay out the required compensation
before a meeting of dozens of villagers, his rivals elected to
use kntves to hack htm and four or bts supporters to death.
Three others were seriously wounded, and not content wtth
that somewhat unorthodox method of repayment, they then
took to burning up to twenty mud-and-thatch huts.
Police later   arrested seven or the villagers on murder
charzes.

                  A HALLOWEEN FOAF--TALE
Twtllght at HaUoween.
A btUng wind danced among the leaftess trees and sent acorns rolling In tbe gutters. The air was brittle and somehow
blade sharp: the snap of a branch In the stillnes of a wtldwood. A bird pecking truitlessly at a shuttered window. Or a
child's htgb-pitcbed laupter, so hysterical it ver&es on a scream.
As sundown came, a cold orange line lit the horizon like the stratpt edte that runs between day and night. And when that
too had ftnally winked out, a pale moon rose, peering between gaps In the ragged cloud at the bare earth below: a
nocturnal land of dark shadows and darker dreams.
Twlllght at Halloween.  •••




Eddle Parsons stood at the entrance to New Ferry Secondary School, closed down these past four months and due for
demolition any day now, feeling part-way foolish and not a Ilttle terrttled. He hunched hls shoulders against the bltlng
